- **rolepod-dblab**: 5 MCP tools (rolepod_db_introspect/query/explain/migrate_verify/write) + 5 user-invocable skills. The data-layer limb of rolepod: introspect schema, assert DB state as verify-evidence, read query plans, detect schema drift (SQLAlchemy vs live), and mutate data behind a transaction preview→confirm→commit/rollback guard. Works standalone with a connection string; pair with the `rolepod` parent plugin (detected via the marker file `&lt;git-root&gt;/.rolepod/parent-active`) and dblab becomes the DB evidence/gate provider — evidence routes to `&lt;git-root&gt;/.rolepod/evidence/` with `manifest.json` per Exte… ([nuttaruj/rolepod-dblab](https://github.com/nuttaruj/rolepod-dblab) · 5 skills, 1 MCP config)
- **rolepod-uiproof**: 33 MCP tools (24 atomic browser/mobile primitives + 9 composite workflows) + 9 user-invocable skills. Works standalone today; pair with the `rolepod` parent plugin (detected via the marker file `&lt;git-root&gt;/.rolepod/parent-active`) and uiproof becomes the verify-phase UI provider — evidence routes to `&lt;git-root&gt;/.rolepod/evidence/` with `manifest.json` per Extension Protocol v1. Replaces chrome-devtools-mcp and playwright-mcp for UI testing. Web production-ready via Playwright; mobile (iOS/Android) via Appium scaffolded — see `rolepod-uiproof doctor` for readiness. ([nuttaruj/rolepod-uiproof](https://github.com/nuttaruj/rolepod-uiproof/tree/HEAD/plugins/rolepod-uiproof) · ★4 · 9 skills, 1 MCP config)
- **rolepod-wplab**: 89+ MCP tools (rolepod_wp_*) + 14 lean Rolepod-pattern skills (/wp-pair-setup, /wp-connect, /wp-health-check, /wp-content, /wp-edit-design, /wp-edit-theme, /wp-edit-plugin, /wp-scaffold, /wp-introspect, /wp-diagnose, /wp-migrate, /wp-changes, /wp-execute-php) + /wp-full alias. 4 connection kinds (local / REST + App Password / SSH / Docker).
